# Signed cyclic kernels and the unpaid actual source response

Status: JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL DRAFT, max3 round50, 2026-10-01.
This is the one analytical round after completed49 authorized by TASK.md and
master_guidance1fc56054c08bd0ac. Completed ceiling50, attempted ceiling51.
No numerical evaluation, trajectory replay, solver, symbolic program, benchmark
DP, fact_submit, installation, consult, or subordinate agent was used.

The actual terminal comparison remains OPEN. The new kernel estimates and the
source obstruction below are draft mathematics, not verifier-accepted facts.
All needed ingredients are proved jointly. The two immutable object sources
37ec6ea9a52bfbdf and66ab91686f1b656f are specifications and awareness, not
mathematical predecessors. Their SHA256 values were confirmed respectively as
a59effa1d6ff0c1ef15ced41f7eedc4b6fbc9028f5bcd0f5eab1cf5740906a0d and
3880b0126b514999ca8623850305cdf23cd5cc27bab8d212b590d65e4412493e.
The sole accepted mathematical input used below is the unconditional benchmark
ray enclosure in fact04219554e41d9750. No conditional seed is presumed to exist.

The results of this single signed-kernel mechanism are:

* Every original terminal LINEAR suffix is an exact Euclidean contraction,
  despite its possibly signed convolution coefficients.
* For the complete original suffix H_(m-1), its actual N-cycle kernel g obeys
  N||g||_2^2<241/216, ||g||_1<17/16, and sum_i(-g_i)_+<1/32.
  Its exact operator norm from l1 to l2 is ||g||_2.
* Taking absolute values of individual factors instead gives an envelope of
  mass greater than5^(2^(m-3))/3^(m-1). This quantifies the discarded cancellation.
* The exact actual nonlinear source response Gamma_m is defined in (25).
  The actual terminal energy satisfies sqrt(J_m)<17/8+Gamma_m. A proof of
  limsup Gamma_m<=3 would imply an eventual actual-family benchmark ratio<9/16.
  That source inequality is NOT proved here.
* A proposed diagonal squared-source payment already fails on the first TWO
  actual updates from -Dv. The exact positive excess is (35). It is not a
  counterexample to the final coherent-source inequality.

The final missing inequality is written in full in Section10. There is no
claim that a stable linear polynomial proves a nonlinear terminal bound.

## 1. The unchanged law and true normalization

Fix an integer m>=8, and set

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta=1-1/m, c=lambda(1+1/m), a=beta/c,
    t_j=1+a*c^j, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,t_j,H_j),
    M=m-1, ell=2^M-1, n=2^m-1,
    d=t_(m-1)=1+beta*c^(m-2), N=ceil(pi*sqrt(d/6)),
    theta=pi/(2N), mu=sin(theta)^2, z=cos(theta),
    v_i=cos((2i+1)theta), 0<=i<N.                         (1)

All embedded words use this SAME enclosing m. The cycle has the directed edges
i to i+1 modulo N, all of weight one. On real vertex vectors set

    (D x)_i=x_i-x_(i+1), L_e=D D^T,
    F(x)=||(D x)_+||_2^2/8,
    T_t(x)=x-(t/4)D^T(Dx)_+.                            (2)

Positive and negative parts are coordinatewise: u_+=max(u,0), u_-=(-u)_+.
The norm ||.||_p is the ordinary finite-dimensional lp norm; inner products
are Euclidean. An identity matrix is denoted I. The declared smoothness is1.

Indeed, the derivative of u_+^2/2 is u_+, and hence
grad F(x)=D^T(Dx)_+/4. Since ||D||<=2 by summing
(x_i-x_(i+1))^2<=2x_i^2+2x_(i+1)^2, and positive part is1-Lipschitz,
grad F is1-Lipschitz. Convexity follows from the scalar summands. The minimum
is zero, and F(x)=0 implies x_i<=x_(i+1) all around the cycle, so its full
minimizer set is span{1}. The gradient has zero sum and every update preserves
the mean. Pairing i with N-1-i proves sum_i v_i=0. The sine-difference identity

    2sin(pi/N)cos((2i+1)pi/N)
      =sin((2i+2)pi/N)-sin(2i*pi/N)

and cos^2 u=(1+cos(2u))/2 give

    R^2=dist(v,argmin F)^2=||v||_2^2=N/2.               (3)

The full original trajectory starts at v and follows H_m. No amplitude,
smoothness, radius, graph, or step schedule is changed below.

For completeness every original directed interpolation row is retained. For
any x,w, with r=(Dx)_+,s=(Dw)_+, scalar sign checking gives

 F(x)-F(w)+<grad F(x),w-x>+||grad F(x)-grad F(w)||_2^2/2
   =-sum_i r_i(-(Dw)_i)_+/4
    -sum_i[(r_i-s_i)+(r_(i+1)-s_(i+1))]^2/32<=0.       (4)

To check (4), start from
phi(u)-phi(v)+u_+(v-u)+(u_+-v_+)^2/2=-u_+(-v)_+,
where phi(u)=u_+^2/2. Sum at u=(Dx)_i/2,v=(Dw)_i/2 and subtract the
gradient-norm correction. The identity
<q,(I-L_e/4)q>=sum_i(q_i+q_(i+1))^2/4 gives the displayed result.
This covers ties, nonlocal pairs, both terminal directions and optimum rows.

## 2. Inherited suffix and elementary parameter bounds

We will use

  12/5<c<11/4<3, 1/4<a<1/2, 7/8<=beta<1,
  1<t_0<3/2, t_1=2-1/m<2, t_2=1+lambda(1-1/m^2)>3.   (5)

For the upper bound on c, lambda<22/9 and1+1/m<=9/8 suffice.
The lower bound on a follows from a=(m-1)/((m+1)lambda)>=7/(9lambda)>7/27>1/4.
The remaining bounds follow directly from (1), lambda>12/5, and lambda>2.

Also

    d>144, N>12, theta<1/8, mu<1/96,
    5/4<mu*d<3/2, mu*t_j<2/3 for j<=m-2.              (6)

Here d>1+(7/8)(12/5)^6>1+(7/8)13^2>144. Put b=pi*sqrt(d/6).
Since pi>sqrt(6), b>sqrt(d)>12 and
b<=N<b+1<(13/12)b. Thus theta^2<1/96. Integration of
cos u>=1-u^2/2 gives sin(theta)/theta>=1-theta^2/6>99/100. It follows that
mu*d<3/2 and mu*d>(3/2)(12/13)^2(99/100)^2>5/4;
the last comparison is2117016>2112500. Finally
t_(m-2)/d=1/c+(1-1/c)/d<5/12+1/144=61/144, giving the last bound.

On the positive ray through v the active edges are0,...,N-2. Cosine addition
at interior vertices and at the two path endpoints gives grad F(v)=mu*v.
The last inequality in (6) therefore proves, by induction on the actual
physical updates in the first child, that

    A_m=product_(j=0)^(m-2)(1-mu*t_j)^(2^(m-2-j))>0,
    x_after_first_child=A_m*v,
    x_after_central_step=-b_m*v,
    b_m=A_m*(mu*d-1)>0.                                (7)

The multiplicities follow from the recursive word, and the induction justifies
regrouping these scalar factors only during this first, unchanged sector.

Normalize the actual inherited suffix by its positive factor b_m: let X_0=-v
and let X_k follow H_M using (2). Define actual edge states

    y_k=D X_k, r_k=(y_k)_+, n_k=(-y_k)_+,
    h_k=(H_M)_k=t_(nu_2(k+1)), 0<=k<ell,
    J_m=N||r_ell||_2^2.                                (8)

Here nu_2(u) is the nonnegative integer power of2 dividing the positive
integer u. The formula for h_k follows by induction on the two children and
their central index. Positive homogeneity of (2) implies that the actual full
terminal point is b_m*X_ell. Its normalized value is exactly

    V_m=2F(b_m*X_ell)/R^2=b_m^2*J_m/(2N^2).             (9)

This normalization does not reset a child state. The actual normalized suffix
has r_0=2z*e_(N-1), where e_i denotes the edge-coordinate unit vector.

## 3. Real cyclic polynomials and exact aliasing

Let S be the cyclic shift on edge vectors, (S f)_i=f_(i-1). Direct multiplication
of D and D^T yields L_e=2I-S-S^(-1). Consequently

    P_t=I-(t/4)L_e=p_t(S),
    p_t(w)=1-t/2+(t/4)(w+w^(-1)).                      (10)

The indeterminate w is unrelated to any state. All products are in the REAL
Laurent-polynomial ring modulo w^N-1. If an ordinary Laurent product has
coefficients c_q, -L<=q<=L, its actual N-cycle kernel is exactly

    g_i=sum_(q congruent i mod N)c_q, 0<=i<N.           (11)

Formula (11) sums every alias, including repeated windings. No relation
between N and a power of2 is assumed. Multiplication becomes cyclic
convolution, (g*f)_i=sum_j g_j f_(i-j), so aliases are taken after every
product or after the full product with identical results. For a new factor
the cyclic coefficients obey

    g'_i=(1-t/2)g_i+(t/4)(g_(i-1)+g_(i+1)).             (12)

Equation (12) is an identity for LINEAR kernels, not the positive-part state
update. Its central coefficient may be negative.

For any original chronological interval [u,v) of physical indices in H_M,
the exact multiplicity of level j is

 c_j[u,v)=floor(v/2^j)-floor(u/2^j)
           -floor(v/2^(j+1))+floor(u/2^(j+1)).          (13)

Indeed it counts the integers u<q<=v divisible by2^j but not2^(j+1).
Its linear Laurent product is product_j p_(t_j)(w)^(c_j[u,v)). This regrouping
is valid because all P_t are polynomials in the same shift. It does not
commute or rearrange the nonlinear physical maps.

For 0<=l<N set xi_l=sin^2(pi*l/N). The characters
f_i=exp(2pi*sqrt(-1)*l*i/N) have L_e/4 eigenvalue xi_l. Define the unnormalized
Fourier transform by hat f_l=sum_i f_i exp(-2pi*sqrt(-1)*l*i/N).
The finite geometric series gives character orthogonality, inversion with
factor1/N, and

    N||f||_2^2=sum_l|hat f_l|^2.                        (14)

The multiplier of (13) is product_j(1-t_j*xi_l)^(c_j[u,v)). Thus the exact
norm of the corresponding operator from l2 to l2 is the largest multiplier
modulus. If g is its coefficient vector, its exact l1-to-l2 norm is ||g||_2:
triangle inequality applied to shifted copies of g gives the upper bound,
and a unit impulse attains it. These identities already include (11).
They involve real coefficients and complex characters only as a basis;
no finite-field inference is used.

## 4. A uniform scalar bound for every complete child

Define, for real x in[0,1],

    q_k(x)=product_(j=0)^(k-1)(1-t_j*x)^(2^(k-1-j)),
    q_0(x)=1, q_(k+1)(x)=q_k(x)^2*(1-t_k*x),
    S_k=sum H_k.                                      (15)

The symbols q_k and S_k in this draft are scalar child products and physical
word sums. In particular B_n is reserved for the original benchmark.

For every k>=0 and0<x<=1 we claim

    |q_k(x)|<=min{1,1/(beta*c^k*x)}.                   (16)

First S_1=1+a>4beta/3=(4/3)a*c, because c<3 and beta<1. If
S_k>=(4/3)a*c^k, then
S_(k+1)=2S_k+1+a*c^k>=(11/3)a*c^k+1>(4/3)a*c^(k+1),
using c<11/4. Thus S_k>=(4/3)a*c^k for every k>=1. With e=exp(1), set

    kappa=3c/(4e)<99/128<4/5,                         (17)

where e>8/3. We prove (16) by three exhaustive sign cases, with exact roots
giving q_k=0 and k=0 giving the claim directly.

If all t_j*x<1 for j<k, then 1-u<=exp(-u) gives
0<q_k(x)<=exp(-S_k*x)<=1/(e*S_k*x)<=kappa/(beta*c^k*x),
as well as q_k<=1. The maximum of u exp(-u) is1/e, by differentiation.

If t_0*x>1, then all factors are negative and
|1-t_j*x|=a*c^j*x-(1-x)<=a*c^j*x. The multiplicity identities

    sum_(j<k)2^(k-1-j)=2^k-1,
    sum_(j<k)j*2^(k-1-j)=2^k-k-1

follow by induction. They give
|q_k(x)|<=(beta*x)^(2^k-1)*c^(-k). This is at most1, and after multiplying
by beta*c^k*x it is at most(beta*x)^(2^k)<=1.

In the remaining case let J>=1 be the first index with t_J*x>1, and put
p=k-J>=1. All factors in q_J are positive. Using the same multiplicity
identities on the tail and |1-t_j*x|<=a*c^j*x there gives

 |q_k(x)|
   <=q_J(x)^(2^p)*(a*c^J*x)^(2^p-1)*c^(2^p-p-1)
   =[a*c^(J+1)*x*q_J(x)]^(2^p)/(beta*c^k*x)
   <=kappa^(2^p)/(beta*c^k*x).                         (18)

For the final inequality, q_J<=exp(-S_J*x) and
a*c^(J+1)*x*exp(-S_J*x)<=a*c^(J+1)/(e*S_J)<=kappa.
Also x<1/t_(J-1)<=1/(1+a), whereas t_J*x>1 implies
a*c^J*x>1-x>a/(1+a)>1/5. Consequently
beta*c^k*x>c^(p+1)/5>=c^2/5>144/125>1.
This proves both parts of (16) in the remaining case. Values at endpoints
and exact roots follow directly or by continuity.

The stronger factor kappa^(2^p) in (18) remains available. No ordering of
nonlinear states or positive kernel coefficient is needed for this estimate.

## 5. Binary terminal suffixes and exact internal contraction

Define the block multiplier

    b_j(x)=(1-t_j*x)q_j(x).                            (19)

It represents the actual block (t_j,H_j) of length2^j. If t_j*x<=1,
(16) gives |b_j(x)|<=1. If t_j*x>1, then
|1-t_j*x|<=a*c^j*x and (16) give |b_j(x)|<=1/c<1.
The same argument works for j=0, since q_0=1 and beta*x<1.
At x=0 every b_j equals1.

Write a remaining length R_rem,0<=R_rem<=2^M-1, in binary. The terminal suffix of
H_M of length R_rem is the concatenation of (t_j,H_j) over its nonzero binary
digits, in INCREASING j. To prove this, if R_rem<2^(M-1), the suffix is within
the final H_(M-1) and induction applies. If R_rem=2^(M-1)+r, its last block is
(t_(M-1),H_(M-1)), preceded by a terminal length-r suffix of the first
H_(M-1). This is exactly the next nonzero binary digit. The empty case starts
the induction. In particular the product of all blocks j<k is q_k.

Let k_R_rem(x)=product_(j:bit_j(R_rem)=1)b_j(x), including k_0=1, and let K_R_rem be its
actual N-cycle operator. The preceding proof gives

    |k_R_rem(x)|<=1 on[0,1], ||K_R_rem||_(2 to2)=1.    (20)

Equality of the norm follows from the constant vector. This is an exact sharp
operator bound for EVERY terminal linear suffix. It does not assert l1 or
order-preserving contraction, and it does not apply (20) to nonlinear maps.

There is also a bounded Laplacian-weighted internal factor. After an actual
step of level j, the remaining word begins with H_j, followed by some higher
blocks. Hence its multiplier contains q_j times factors of modulus<=1.
Using (16), for every x in[0,1],

 t_j*x*|k_(ell-k-1)(x)|
   <=t_j*x*|q_j(x)|<=1/c+1/(beta*c^j)
   <5/12+8/7=131/84<8/5,
       when h_k=t_j.                                 (21)

This controls an individual term in the summation-by-parts formula below;
it is not a bound on the sum of actual negative-part states.

## 6. Full-kernel smoothing, small negative mass, and lost cancellation

Let g be the kernel of K_ell, the full H_M linear product. The actual rounding
law gives, using (6), pi^2<10 and c>12/5,

 N^2/(beta*c^M)
   <[169*pi^2/(864*c)]*d/(d-1)
   <8450/10296<5/6.                                   (22)

For 1<=l<N put q=min(l,N-l). Concavity of sine on[0,pi/2] gives
sin(pi*q/N)>=2q/N. From (16), |q_M(xi_l)|<5/(24q^2).
At frequency0 the multiplier is1. There are at most two frequencies for
each q. Since sum_(q>=1)q^(-4)<1+integral_1^infinity u^(-4)du=4/3,
(14) yields

    N||g||_2^2=sum_l|q_M(xi_l)|^2<241/216,
    ||g||_1<=sqrt(N)||g||_2<sqrt(241/216)<17/16,
    sum_i(-g_i)_+=(||g||_1-1)/2<1/32.                 (23)

The last identity uses sum_i g_i=q_M(0)=1. It permits signed coefficients;
it neither asserts nor requires any negative coefficient actually to occur.
In particular, the exact linear propagation of the actual positive initial
part has

    N||K_ell*r_0||_2^2=4z^2*N||g||_2^2<241/54,
    sqrt(N)||K_ell*r_0||_2<17/8.                       (24)

For comparison, take absolute values of every individual factor in (10)
before multiplying. Its nonnegative envelope has total mass1 for t<=2,
and t-1 for t>2. Using (5), the full envelope's mass for H_M is exactly

 product_(j=2)^(M-1)(t_j-1)^(2^(M-1-j))
   =(beta*c^2)^(2^(M-2))/(beta*c^M)
   >5^(2^(M-2))/3^M.

The count of the exponents is2^(M-2)-1 and their j-weighted sum is
3*2^(M-2)-M-1; substitution of t_j-1=a*c^j gives the identity. Finally
beta*c^2>(7/8)(12/5)^2=126/25>5 and beta*c^M<3^M prove the strict bound.
Since the envelope coefficients are nonnegative, folding onto the ACTUAL
cycle by (11) leaves this mass unchanged. In terms of m the last lower bound
is5^(2^(m-3))/3^(m-1), whereas the signed kernel itself has mass norm<17/16.
An estimate based on that product of absolute factors loses this much
cancellation even before any nonlinear source is priced.

## 7. Exact actual sources: signs, support, amplitudes and Duhamel

At step k put alpha=h_k/4 and w_i=alpha(r_(k,i-1)+r_(k,i+1)). The original
positive-part edge update, obtained by applying D to (2), is

    y_(k+1,i)=y_(k,i)-2alpha*r_(k,i)+w_i.

Define the absorption vector abs_k and the repair vector rep_k by

 abs_(k,i)=1_(y_(k,i)<=0)*min(n_(k,i),w_i),
 rep_(k,i)=1_(y_(k,i)>0)*[(2alpha-1)r_(k,i)-w_i]_+,
 s_k=rep_k-abs_k.

Both vectors are nonnegative and their supports are disjoint. Absorption
occurs only on old strictly negative edges adjacent to an old positive edge;
at an old zero its amplitude is zero. Its amplitude is at most both the
old negative depth n_(k,i) and the incoming amount w_i. Repair occurs only
on old positive edges that become strictly negative. It vanishes if h_k<=2,
and satisfies rep_(k,i)<=max(h_k/2-1,0)r_(k,i). Thus no large-step increase
or sign change has been omitted. Exact ties give zero on the appropriate
boundary and require no choice of active-set convention.

Checking the two old sign cases gives the identities

    r_(k+1)=P_(h_k)r_k+s_k,
    n_(k+1)=n_k+s_k.

On an old nonpositive coordinate P r=w>=0 and r'=(w-n)_+=w-min(w,n).
On an old positive coordinate n=0 and taking the positive part adds exactly
(-P r)_+. This proves both identities, including ties.

There is useful local dissipativity: writing v_lin=P_(h_k)r_k, we have v_lin=-rep_k
on the repair support and v_lin>=abs_k on the absorption support. Therefore

 ||r_(k+1)||_2^2-||v_lin||_2^2
   =-||rep_k||_2^2-2<v_lin,abs_k>+||abs_k||_2^2
   <=-||rep_k||_2^2-||abs_k||_2^2.

This does not assert ||v_lin||<=||r_k|| at large steps. Also sum y_k=0 at all
times, so if M_k=sum_i r_(k,i), then
M_(k+1)-M_k=sum rep_k-sum abs_k. The repair term is not set to zero.

Iterating the actual affine-in-r identity, retaining each source where its
actual state generates it, gives

 r_ell=K_ell*r_0+sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)K_(ell-k-1)*s_k,
 Gamma_m=sqrt(N)||sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)K_(ell-k-1)*s_k||_2.
                                                               (25)

The multiplication symbol here denotes an operator acting on a vector, or
equivalently convolution with its cyclic kernel. In particular the last
source has the IDENTITY propagator K_0. No source moves to a different time.
By (24) and triangle inequality this gives the unconditional actual estimate

    sqrt(J_m)<17/8+Gamma_m.                            (26)

This is a paid linear term plus an EXPLICIT unpaid nonlinear source term.
For clarity the entirely resolved Fourier expression for that source term is

 Gamma_m^2=sum_(l=0)^(N-1)
    |sum_(k=0)^(ell-1) k_(ell-k-1)(xi_l)
              [hat rep_(k,l)-hat abs_(k,l)]|^2.         (27)

The inner sums retain all source phases and signs. Their kernels are fixed
polynomials from (15),(19); only the actual trajectory generates the sources.

There are two useful exact alternative ways to inspect the missing payment.
First, expanding the square in (25) includes every term
<s_j,K_(ell-j-1)K_(ell-k-1)s_k>. Although these matrices commute and are
symmetric, these cross terms cannot be discarded; Section8 gives an actual
counterexample. Second, s_k=n_(k+1)-n_k gives summation by parts:

 sum_k K_(ell-k-1)*s_k
   =n_ell-K_ell*n_0
       -sum_k (h_k/4)L_e K_(ell-k-1)*n_k.              (28)

For verification, the direct telescoping has endpoint -K_(ell-1)n_0 and
interior differences K_(ell-k)-K_(ell-k-1)
=-(h_k/4)L_e K_(ell-k-1). Adding the k=0 term converts the endpoint to
-K_ell n_0. At frequency zero (28) is exactly M_ell-M_0, since
sum n_k=sum r_k. At other frequencies (21) bounds each weighted operator
but provides no cancellation or summability of its entire actual history.
Equations (27) and (28) identify the missing information without changing
the physical chronology.

## 8. Two direct source-payment tests and their precise failures

### 8.1 Diagonal square payment fails on the prescribed actual prefix

One tempting repair of (27) is to use contraction of all propagators to claim
the squared response is bounded by the sum of squared source norms. Even the
first two actual physical steps from -Dv refute this proposed general lemma.

Relabel the closing edge N-1 as spatial0 and its neighbors by signed indices.
This changes labels only. Write u=t_0/4,w=t_1/4. From (5),(6),

    1/4<u<3/8, 15/32<=w<1/2, mu<1/96.

Cosine subtraction for the SAME initial vector gives

 r_0=2z e_0, y_(0,+-1)=-4mu z,
 y_(0,+-2)=-8mu z(1-2mu).

All other noncentral initial edges are negative. The first step leaves the
central edge positive and activates exactly its two neighbors, with
r_(1,+-1)=z(2u-4mu)>0. Therefore

    abs_0=A(e_1+e_-1), rep_0=0, A=4mu z.              (29)

At the second step all three old positives stay positive because w<1/2.
The next two edges activate, since

 w(2u-4mu)>(15/32)(11/24)=165/768>1/12
                      >8mu(1-2mu).

All other edges have no positive neighbor and remain unchanged and negative.
Thus the exact second correction is

    abs_1=C(e_2+e_-2), rep_1=0,
    C=8mu z(1-2mu)=2A(1-2mu).                         (30)

Let E=e_1+e_-1. The entries of P_(t1)E are1-2w at+-1, w at+-2, and2w at0.
N>12 prevents any alias collision among these named coordinates. Since
s_0=-abs_0,s_1=-abs_1, we obtain

 ||P_(t1)s_0||_2^2=2A^2(1-4w+7w^2),
 <P_(t1)s_0,s_1>=2ACw,
 ||s_0||_2^2=2A^2.                                   (31)

The exact excess in the attempted squared-source payment is consequently

 ||P_(t1)s_0+s_1||_2^2-||s_0||_2^2-||s_1||_2^2
    =2A^2*w*(7w-8mu)>0.                              (32)

The strict inequality follows from7w>=105/32 and8mu<1/12. Both step factors
are genuine Euclidean contractions, because t_0,t_1<2 and their multipliers
are1-t_j*xi_l with0<=xi_l<=1. The local source supports are disjoint, but
propagation makes their negative absorptions overlap coherently.

This is a counterexample on the actual prescribed start and original first
two steps, not a substitute diagnostic state. Its scope is the generic
diagonal source-payment lemma for actual prefixes. The final-horizon weights
in (27) are different, so (32) does NOT disprove limsup Gamma_m<=3.

### 8.2 An absolute source budget needs the actual time-dependent prices

For a terminal length R_rem define the EXACT nonnegative linear price

    gamma_R_rem=[sum_l|k_R_rem(xi_l)|^2]^(1/2)
            =sqrt(N)||kernel(K_R_rem)||_2.

The l1-to-l2 identity in Section3 gives the rigorous sufficient bound

    Gamma_m<=sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)
       gamma_(ell-k-1)(||abs_k||_1+||rep_k||_1).        (33)

This bound loses cancellation between source times, but not cancellation
inside the real kernel. Unlike a claim with unspecified constants, it makes
each price explicit. Replacing every gamma_R_rem by the full-kernel bound17/16
is false on these very same physical suffixes. In fact gamma_0=sqrt(N).
The length-one terminal suffix is the original t_0, and

 gamma_1^2=N[(1-t_0/2)^2+2(t_0/4)^2]
            =N[1-t_0+3t_0^2/8]>=N/3,                 (34)

because the bracket is minimized at t_0=4/3 with value1/3. Thus the final
absorption source and its predecessor need an actual amplitude or cancellation
estimate at their actual late times. The exact sources (29),(30) do not give
such an estimate for those different inherited late states.

The alternative use of (20) gives only
Gamma_m<=sqrt(N)sum_k||s_k||_2. Its proposed diagonal improvement is refuted
by the ACTUAL strict inequality

 N||P_(t1)s_0+s_1||_2^2
   -N(||s_0||_2^2+||s_1||_2^2)=2NA^2*w*(7w-8mu)>0.   (35)

Finally the summation-by-parts attempt (28) retains a different obstacle:
the bounded individual weights (21) are multiplied by all actual n_k,
including newly created negative depths at oversteps. Neither an assumed
absence of those depths nor a sum estimate follows from (20). No scalar
total-clock/mass-cap closure is retried here. The two direct screens locate
the source cross terms and the late absorption amplitudes that are still
uncontrolled within this same mechanism.

## 9. The exact all-split benchmark and a quantitative source threshold

We retain the original benchmark, in the W=2R normalization:

 C_0=B_0=1,
 Omega(s,t)=(s+t+sqrt((s+t)^2+4st))/2,
 G(s,t)=2s+t/2+sqrt(t^2+8st)/2,
 C_n=max_(0<=j<n)Omega(C_j,C_(n-j-1)),
 B_n=max_(0<=j<n)G(C_j,B_(n-j-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

All split indices remain in these definitions. Accepted fact04219554e41d9750,
its unconditional statement and proof Section7, gives
B_(2^m-1)/lambda^m -> Z_1 with0<Z_1<infinity. Its enclosure at b=1,j=0,
and B_1=4 from the original recurrence, give

    Z_1<=(2+3/100)^2=41209/10000<21/5.                (36)

We also need the incoming amplitude asymptotics; these are rederived rather
than imported from an unverified draft. From (1),

 d/lambda^m -> exp(1)/lambda^2,
 N^2/lambda^m -> pi^2*exp(1)/(6lambda^2), mu*d ->3/2.

The first follows by taking logarithms of
(1-1/m)(1+1/m)^(m-2). Bounds s-s^2/2<=log(1+s)<=s show its limit is e.
The additive1 in d and the ceiling error in N are negligible. The elementary
limit sin(theta)/theta->1 proves the last assertion.

Reindex (7) by r=m-1-j to write

 A_m=product_(r=1)^(m-1)
      [1-mu-(mu*d-mu)c^(-r)]^(2^(r-1)).

Every subtracted quantity lies in[0,2/3) by (6), and
-log(1-u)<=3u in this interval by integration. The logarithmic tails are
therefore bounded by the sums of3*2^(r-1)[mu+(3/2)lambda^(-r)]. The total
mu contribution is at most3mu2^(m-1)->0. The remaining tail tends to zero
uniformly with the cutoff because2/lambda<1. Each finite prefix converges.
Hence

 A_m -> A_infty=product_(r>=1)
         [1-(3/2)lambda^(-r)]^(2^(r-1))>0,
 b_m -> A_infty/2.                                    (37)

Each limiting factor is positive and the absolutely convergent log series
proves the strict positivity. Combining (9),(36),(37) gives

 B_(2^m-1)V_m=K_m J_m,
 K_m=b_m^2*B_(2^m-1)/(2N^2)
       ->K_cycle=3lambda^2*A_infty^2*Z_1/(4pi^2*e)>0. (38)

There is a useful elementary upper bound on this unchanged coefficient.
Retain just the first THREE factors of A_infty. Since
lambda<5/2, lambda^2<6, and lambda^3=7+5sqrt2<15,

 lambda^2*A_infty^2
   <=(lambda-3/2)^2
      [1-3/(2lambda^2)]^4[1-3/(2lambda^3)]^8
   <1*(3/4)^4*(9/10)^8<81/512.

The last comparison uses (9/10)^4=6561/10000<2/3, whose square is less
than1/2. Since pi^2>9 and e>8/3, (36) proves

    0<K_cycle<5103/245760<1/48.                       (39)

No approximation to a balanced benchmark replaces the exact B_n here.

We choose the ONE quantitative source estimate

                    limsup_(m->infinity) Gamma_m<=3. (40)

If (40) were proved for the actual sources in Section7, (26),(38),(39) would
give

 limsup_(m->infinity) B_(2^m-1)V_m
   <=K_cycle*(17/8+3)^2
   <1681/3072<9/16.                                  (41)

In particular this actual cycle family would eventually have
V_m<(9/16)U_(2^m-1)^ZJ. This would be an upper deficit for this ONE family,
not an upper bound on the unrestricted W_n. Conversely, no consequence of
(41) is asserted without (40). Linear smoothing alone establishes (24),
not (40). A finite horizon proving (40) or any limiting actual J_m is absent.

## 10. The remaining inequality, written independently of shorthand

For every enclosing m>=8 fix exactly the data (1),(2), start the inherited
suffix at y_0=-Dv, and use the original h_k=t_(nu_2(k+1)) for0<=k<ell.
Generate y_(k+1)=y_k-(h_k/4)L_e(y_k)_+. Set r_k=(y_k)_+, n_k=(-y_k)_+,
w_(k,i)=(h_k/4)(r_(k,i-1)+r_(k,i+1)), and

 abs_(k,i)=1_(y_(k,i)<=0)min(n_(k,i),w_(k,i)),
 rep_(k,i)=1_(y_(k,i)>0)[(h_k/2-1)r_(k,i)-w_(k,i)]_+.

Let the Fourier transform and xi_l be exactly as in Section3, q_j and b_j
exactly as in (15),(19). The one selected unresolved source inequality is

 limsup_(m->infinity) sum_(l=0)^(N-1)
   |sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)
       [product_(j:bit_j(ell-k-1)=1)(1-t_j*xi_l)q_j(xi_l)]
       [hat rep_(k,l)-hat abs_(k,l)]|^2 <=9.            (42)

There are no free states, graph weights, horizons or amplitudes in (42).
It retains every source sign and all finite-cycle aliases. It is equivalent
to (40). This draft neither proves (42) nor refutes it. It supplies the new
kernel estimates, the explicit benchmark-relevant price that is missing,
and an actual counterexample to a particular attempted payment (35).

## 11. Retrieval, status and handoff

The query-memory, direct-proving, search-math-results and construct-counterexamples
skills were used. Fact searches located no verified actual-cycle kernel or
terminal-energy result. Targeted Matlas queries returned statements on other
evolution or control systems; none was used. A web fallback located and led
to reading the exact primary paper by Ketcheson, Loczi and Parsani,
*Propagation of internal errors in explicit Runge--Kutta methods and internal
stability of SSP and extrapolation methods*, arXiv:1309.1317v3 (2014).
The PDF was downloaded in the worker's downloads directory. Local extraction
reported "No module named 'pypdf'"; the exact PDF's text was read using the
web PDF extractor, including Section2.1, Theorem2.3 and Theorem3.1 with proofs.

The paper distinguishes the endpoint polynomial from polynomials transporting
stage residuals. Its normal-matrix argument motivates retaining the complete
source response. The nonlinear contractivity theorem requires a stable
forward-Euler condition and nonnegative convex stage combinations; neither is
available merely from our large physical steps. No paper theorem is imported:
the cyclic spectral and Duhamel arguments are proved directly here.

The detailed source screens are (35), which disproves a diagonal squared-norm
payment on an ACTUAL prefix, and (34), which excludes a uniform full-kernel
impulse price for the late actual suffixes. Neither decides (42), the actual
cycle family, or the original all-word/all-horizon exact-profile theorem.
The finite numerical report037f4a01133b38bc is not an asymptotic premise.

During closeout, shared finding336be1a25a3c10bc reported max4's distinct draft
failure of the unit free-heat profile at the fourth actual update. It was read
as shared awareness only and is not used in any proof here. This draft's
signed terminal-operator bounds do not assert that pointwise profile envelope.

The preliminary kernel findingea36215055cf165e and source counterexample
0de349e50e4e530f are contained with their proofs in this joint draft. To avoid
collision with the benchmark notation, this document calls their scalar
products q_k and b_j; the preliminary finding's locally scoped Q_k and B_j
were the same scalar products, not benchmark denominators.

New accepted facts:0. Formal calls:0. New mathematical computations:0.
The actual terminal source inequality and original target remain OPEN.
This ends the one allocated analytical mechanism/round, with no automatic
renewal, publication, finalization, revocation or service shutdown.

# max2 invocation61: signed word-dependent nonuniform incidence memory

ONE allocated analytical round complete. All new mathematics is JOINT
UNVERIFIED. Original exact-profile Goal remains OPEN. No new fact,
formal call, mathematical program or automatic renewal.

Shared records:

## Actual new construction and geometry

The three frozen laws use a projective plane over a prime field, but
EVERY metric operation is real. Point weights depend on the FULL word:

    omega_P=1+1_(P=P0)
            +sum_(r<floor(k/2))b_r 1_(P in L_r)/(1+S).

Here b lists the positive physical steps, S=sum h and k is their count.
All original zero-step samples are restored as identical jets. Weights
are positive and nonuniform. With W=diag(omega), P_w projection
perpendicular to the square-root weight vector and A the complete real
weighted centered feature matrix normalized by sqrt(p_wm*max omega),

    A A^T=P_w W P_w/max omega.

This is proved using the whole field, not a selected covariance guess.
The exact Gram uses weighted line intersections and weighted degrees.
The standalone appendix proves that the complete Gram is regular-simplex
if and only if every point weight is constant; hence the new geometry
leaves the old simplex hypothesis for EVERY applicable word.

Retired suffix memories are normalized by their exact positive real norm.
Their early and late coefficients use b_r and b_(k-1-r), and the late
coefficient is explicitly negative. All original positions, gradient and
value arrays, full initial correlations and real Gram are written in the
joint file. Every original finite, optimum, terminal and remote row has
an exact formula. Original first-cycle coefficients and the complete
initial-optimum-to-terminal path are derived anew, not imported from
the previous fixed laws.

## Three exact scoped screens

1. Transverse balanced return, frozen(lambda_echo,c_echo,theta_echo)=(0,1,1):
   sum h*g=S*g_n, but g0=g_n+sigma*u0 with u0 perpendicular to g_n.
   The REMOTE initial-terminal cycle is sigma^2>0 for every positive
   scale and every k>=2. A planar attempted transcript passes all four
   adjacent directed rows AND all six optimum rows; Q02=3/2 and
   Q20=-1/2 still violate full interpolation. It is not a legal function.

2. Longitudinally compensated balanced return,(1,1,1):
   a legal static nonzero-memory finite example exists. Nevertheless the
   exact full displacement remains sum h*g=S*g_n. At a TRUE closest
   minimizer x_cl, the original terminal row gives

       2(f_n-f*)<=2<g_n,X_echo-x_cl>-(1+2S)||g_n||^2
                  <=||X_echo-x_cl||^2/(1+2S).

   Thus every legal member, ALL anchors and ALL common values, has
   true-radius payoff<=Huber. At actual n=2^m-1 on the unchanged
   enclosing-m tau1/4 H_m,m>=1536, accepted5fd396eba75651e7 gives
   ratio<237/250. This is a witness-family ceiling, not W upper.

3. Over-return2 with transverse amplitude1/2,(1,1/2,2):
   total displacement does not close, and a concrete static two-step
   bridge has payoff8/53>Huber1/9 and>actual U2. It is below the existing
   single-cut1/5. On EVERY positive palindrome with endpoint b<5/3,
   the last queried gradient is sigma*(-e0-u0), terminal gradient
   sigma*e0, and the LAST actual two-row cycle is sigma^2*(5-3b)>0.
   The unchanged H_m has b<sqrt2<5/3, defeating the entire stated
   scale family at every assigned depth. Initial-anchor/value changes
   cannot fix that cycle. Other endpoint ratios are outside this scope.

## Two static finite bridges, paid radius and every row

Both use word(2,2), sigma1 and orthonormal(e0,u0) coordinates. The
main file displays the COMPLETE four-by-four original residual matrices
including the optimum, and explicit fixed conjugates K_B,K_C whose
conjugates give ONE function each. Each K is quadratic near0, so its
conjugate is sharp1-smooth with UNIQUE minimizer0.

Balanced B:

    x:(6,-1),(2,-3),(2,-1);
    g:(2,1),(0,-1),(1,0);
    f:13/2,5/2,3/2.
    True squared radius37; payoff3/37<1/9.

Over-retired C:

    x:(7/2,-1),(-1/2,-2),(3/2,0);
    g:(2,1/2),(-1,-1),(1,0);
    f:15/4,3/2,1.
    True squared radius53/4; payoff8/53.

The negative middle initial-radius component and gradient correlation
in C are actual legal finite data. They are not expected-feasibility
or an uninstantiated PSD direction. These new claims are still drafts;
only the verifier can accept them as facts.

## What the next law must change

Exact cancellation to S*g_n cannot improve Huber, even after longitudinal
payment and even at the closest minimum. A new nonclosing return must
pay BOTH its actual remote cycles and its final small-step return.
Terminal memory or different full-word endpoint ratios remain open.
No unrestricted weighted-Gram impossibility follows from these screens.

A scalable all-word construction, word-decomposition argument and
uniform original-B comparison remain THEOREM-LEVEL gaps. A finite
bridge or this diagnostic-family failure does not settle the original
every-n/every-h asymptotic target. There is no original-target candidate

# Signed word-dependent incidence memory with a nonuniform real metric

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Worker max2,
invocation61, plan72d7a01f0dbb60d2, master_guidance ae616dd4bc25205f,
elaboration d0da7eab62ffb4e1. The original exact-profile target is OPEN.
This is the ONE allocated analytical invocation after ended baseline60,
with ended ceiling61/attempted ceiling62. It does not renew itself.

No mathematical program, numerical evaluation, arithmetic replay,
trajectory execution, optimizer, solver, benchmark recurrence or DP,
formal submission, installation, consult or subagent was used. All
finite calculations below are written symbolic derivations. File/JSON
reading, text persistence and integrity hashes are administrative only.

## 1. Results and their exact scopes

Three explicitly specified coefficient laws use the SAME new full-word
weighted incidence geometry and a genuinely signed retired memory.
The real feature Gram is not the old centered regular-simplex Gram.

* A: purely transverse, exactly balanced retirement. Initial and terminal
  gradients differ transversely, but the full actual displacement equals
  minus total step mass times the terminal gradient. The ORIGINAL remote
  two-row cycle (0,n),(n,0) is exactly sigma^2>0. No common values,
  initial anchor, minimum translation or scale selection can realize
  this nonzero-memory law when at least two steps are positive. A written
  two-step diagnostic passes BOTH adjacent cycles and every optimum row;
  the missing remote pair still fails.
* B: longitudinally compensated, exactly balanced retirement. The added
  longitudinal charge makes a fully legal finite example possible, with
  a static sharp1-smooth interpolant, unique minimum and true radius.
  Nevertheless EVERY legal realization of this displacement-closure
  law has true-radius payoff <=1/(1+2S), for ALL words and ALL legal
  common values, including arbitrary initial anchors and minima. On the
  unchanged tau1/4 hierarchy its original-benchmark ratio is <237/250.
* C: fixed over-retirement2 with initial transverse amplitude1/2.
  It leaves exact displacement closure, and one legal static two-step
  example gives 8/53>1/9, even exceeding the actual U_2^ZJ benchmark.
  It is below the accepted same-word single-cut witness1/5. On EVERY
  positive palindrome with endpoint step b<5/3, the LAST actual two-row
  cycle is sigma^2(5-3b)>0. The unchanged tau1/4 hierarchy has b<sqrt(2),
  so the ENTIRE stated scale family fails there at every assigned depth.

These are scoped failures of the THREE displayed laws, not an exclusion
of arbitrary signed memory or weighted geometry. Only B's exact
displacement closure, and C's frozen endpoint ratios, are excluded.
No unrestricted W upper bound or original-target resolution follows.
The finite examples are jointly proved below, NOT verifier-accepted facts.

Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the original smooth-convex inequalities
and the explicit finite-jet conjugate interpolant. Accepted5fd396eba75651e7
is used ONLY for its original-B comparison in Section10. Accepted
b98bb807d514e80d supplies the original benchmark definitions and the
single-cut reference. Every new weighted-incidence identity, coefficient
law, obstruction and finite example is derived in this joint manuscript;
no unverified finding is a mathematical premise.

## 2. Physical words and zero-step repetitions

Normalize L=1. The full given physical word is

    h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1))>=0, S=sum_(t<n)h_t.             (1)

Let k be the number of positive entries, list their original indices
i_0<...<i_(k-1), and write b_r=h_(i_r)>0. Define

    j(i)=number of positive physical steps strictly before i,
                                             0<=i<=n. (2)

Every displayed compressed jet with index r is lifted to original
sample i by r=j(i). This is an ACTUAL witness-array convention: zero
steps repeat the complete point, gradient and value. It imposes no
equality between formal gradient coordinates in an upper certificate.
All original ordered pairs, including all repeated queries, are kept.
There is no deletion of a positive physical step or a terminal row.

For k<2 there is no paired memory. To define the portfolio also there,
use the separate standard symmetric Huber function with threshold
delta=1/(1+2S), initial point1 and unique minimizer0. Its derivative
is max(-delta,min(delta,x)). Nonnegative prefix mass keeps every query
and the terminal on the positive plateau: x_i=1-delta*sum_(t<i)h_t
and x_n=(1+S)/(1+2S)>=delta. Its true-radius payoff is exactly
1/(1+2S). For S=0 the threshold is1 and the unchanged initial point
has payoff1. This fallback repairs no failure at k>=2.

For the three memory laws henceforth k>=2, and ell=floor(k/2).
Pair each early r<ell with r'=k-1-r. If k is odd the middle query
r=ell has zero memory. The terminal query k has zero memory as well.

## 3. Exact whole-field nonuniform real geometry

### 3.1 Explicit projective points, lines and counts

Choose the SMALLEST prime p_wm such that N=p_wm^2+p_wm+1>=k+1. This is a
definition, not a prime search executed in this round. Existence follows
from unboundedness of primes: a prime divisor of the integer
(k+1)!+1 is greater than k+1, since none of the smaller primes divides
that integer. No factorial or factorization was evaluated.

Work over the field Z/p_wm Z. Points are nonzero triples modulo nonzero
scalar multiplication; normalize a representative so its first nonzero
coordinate is1, and order these representatives lexicographically.
Lines are nonzero linear forms modulo nonzero scalar multiplication,
with the SAME representative/ordering convention. A point is on a line
exactly when its representative annihilates that linear form. Let P0
be the point represented by(1,0,0). Choose L_0 to be the line with
form(1,0,0), namely x=0; it does not contain P0. Choose L_1,...,
L_(ell-1) as the first remaining lines in the stated lexicographic order.
Since N>=k+1, these distinct selected lines form a proper subset.

There are N points and N lines: divide p_wm^3-1 nonzero triples by p_wm-1.
One nonzero form has p_wm^2 elements in its kernel, so its line has
(p_wm^2-1)/(p_wm-1)=p_wm+1 points. Two distinct forms are independent and
have a one-dimensional common kernel, hence their lines share one
point. Dually, every point lies on p_wm+1 lines and two distinct points
lie on one common line. These assertions follow by eliminating one
or two coordinates with nonzero coefficients, also for p_wm=2.

Let B be the REAL N-by-N incidence matrix, with point rows and line
columns. The point-side counts give the exact full-field identity

    B B^T=p_wm I+J_ff,                                     (3)

where I is the identity and J_ff is the all-ones matrix. In particular
B is invertible: for nonzero real z,
z^T(p_wm I+J_ff)z=p_wm||z||^2+(sum z)^2>0. Also B*1=(p_wm+1)*1.

### 3.2 Full-word point weights and their exact Gram

Use STRICTLY POSITIVE REAL point weights

    omega_P=1+1_(P=P0)
                 +(1/(1+S))*sum_(r<ell)b_r 1_(P in L_r),
    Omega_w=sum_P omega_P
            =N+1+[(p_wm+1)/(1+S)]*sum_(r<ell)b_r,
    omega_max=max_P omega_P.                          (4)

Thus 1<=omega_P<3. P0 has weight at least2 and every other point has
weight strictly below2, so the weights are nonuniform and P0 is their
unique maximizer. No negative metric weight or finite-field scalar
product is used as a Euclidean norm.

Let W be the real diagonal matrix diag(omega_P), let s have coordinates
sqrt(omega_P), and put P_w=I-s*s^T/Omega_w. This is an orthogonal
projection since ||s||^2=Omega_w. For EVERY projective line L, set

    d_L=sum_(P in L)omega_P,
    z_L(P)=sqrt(omega_P)*(1_(P in L)-d_L/Omega_w),
    A_L=z_L/sqrt(p_wm*omega_max).                        (5)

These are definite real vectors in R^N, perpendicular to s. Their
complete Gram, with no line omitted, is

    K_LM=sum_(P in L intersection M)omega_P-d_L*d_M/Omega_w,
    G_LM=<A_L,A_M>=K_LM/(p_wm*omega_max).                (6)

For L=M the weighted intersection sum is d_L. For L!=M it is the
weight of their unique intersection point. Moreover the degrees are
the fully explicit expressions

    d_L=p_wm+1+1_(P0 in L)
             +[H_pre+p_wm*b_r 1_(L=L_r for r<ell)]/(1+S),
    H_pre=sum_(r<ell)b_r.                             (7)

The bracket uses b_r only if L is a selected line, and otherwise is
H_pre. Formula(7) follows from one-point intersections and the p_wm+1
points on a line meeting itself.

There is an exact WHOLE-FIELD frame normalization. Let A be the matrix
with ALL N feature columns A_L, not only the selected temporal lines.
The unscaled feature matrix is P_w W^(1/2) B. By(3),

    A A^T=P_w W P_w/omega_max<=P_w<=I.               (8)

Indeed P_w W^(1/2) J_ff W^(1/2) P_w=P_w*s*s^T*P_w=0,
and the p_wm I term is p_wm P_w W P_w. Thus the normalization is proved on
the whole field and not guessed from a selected small Gram. For ANY
real temporal coefficient columns c,d,

    <sum_L c_L A_L,sum_M d_M A_M>=c^T G_wm d,        (9)

where G_wm is the matrix with entries G_LM in(6). This is a real PSD
Gram because the vectors
are explicitly Euclidean; no arbitrary PSD selection is substituted.

The only null coefficient column of A is a constant column. In fact,
A c=0 implies W^(1/2)B c is proportional to s=W^(1/2)1, hence
B c is constant; invertibility and B1=(p_wm+1)1 imply c is constant.
This proves nonvanishing of every nonzero coefficient column supported
on a proper subset of the lines, with no independence assumption.

### 3.3 This is not the regular-simplex metric

For the written diagnostic b=(2,2), k=2, ell=1, the smallest p_wm is2,
N=7, and the specified first selected line L0 is x=0, which does not
contain P0.
The weights are2 at P0, 7/5 on L0, and1 on the remaining points.
Their total is46/5. The degrees of L0, of another line through P0,
and of another line not through P0 are respectively21/5,22/5,17/5.
Their squared unscaled feature norms are d*(1-d/Omega_w).
For the first two, the difference is

    (21/5-22/5)*(1-(21/5+22/5)/(46/5))=-3/230!=0.   (10)

So even the feature norms are unequal. The whole real Gram in(6) is
not a constant-diagonal/constant-off-diagonal regular-simplex Gram.
This demonstration uses no numerical evaluation or trajectory replay.

## 4. Signed retired memory and the three definite laws

For early r<ell define the RETIRED SUFFIX, not a fresh independent
gradient innovation,

    eta_a=b_a/(1+S),
    U_r=sum_(a=r)^(ell-1) eta_a A_(L_a),
    R_U=||U_0||>0, Psi_r=U_r/R_U, u0=Psi_0.          (11)

Nonvanishing follows from the exact nullspace argument after(9): U_0
has positive coefficients on a proper subset, zero coefficients on
the other lines, and hence is not in that nullspace. In particular
||u0||=1. The normalization is the exact expression

    R_U^2=sum_(a,b<ell)eta_a eta_b G_(L_a,L_b).        (12)

The exact memory covariances are

    <Psi_r,Psi_s>=(1/R_U^2)
          *sum_(a>=r,b>=s; a,b<ell)eta_a eta_b G_(L_a,L_b).
                                                          (13)

Let e0 be an additional unit vector perpendicular to R^N. Put

    alpha_r=b_(r')/(1+b_r+b_(r')),
    beta_r=b_r/(1+b_r+b_(r')),
    alpha0=alpha_0>0,
    Y_r=(lambda_echo/alpha0)*e0
                           +(c_echo/alpha0)*Psi_r.   (14)

For three FROZEN triples define complete gradient-vector laws

    A: (lambda_echo,c_echo,theta_echo)=(0,1,1),
    B: (lambda_echo,c_echo,theta_echo)=(1,1,1),
    C: (lambda_echo,c_echo,theta_echo)=(1,1/2,2).      (15)

For each triple, for every compressed query r<ell and its pair r',

    v_r^echo=e0+alpha_r Y_r,
    v_(r')^echo=e0-theta_echo*beta_r Y_r,
    v_ell^echo=e0 if k is odd,
    v_k^echo=e0.                                         (16)

The actual initial anchors are

    X_A=X_B=6e0-u0,
    X_C=(7/2)e0-u0.                                  (17)

Let X_echo be the corresponding X_A, X_B or X_C for the chosen law.

All point weights, line selection, suffix memories and signed temporal
coefficients are explicit functions of the FULL given h. In particular
the late coefficient is a NEGATIVE return of the same early incidence
memory; no independence assumption discards the correlation. Every
member uses its fixed data once, not a time-varying choice of function.

For sigma>0 define all compressed and original physical samples by

    D_r=sum_(t<r)b_t v_t^echo,
    xhat_r=X_echo-sigma D_r, ghat_r=sigma v_r^echo,
    x_i=xhat_(j(i)), g_i=ghat_(j(i)).                  (18)

These positions satisfy EVERY physical GD update, including zero steps.
They are symbolic finite sums, not executed trajectories.

### 4.1 Full real gradient Gram, including the initial anchor

Write v_i^echo=L_i e0+Z_i. For paired r,r' set

    t_r=alpha_r, t_(r')=-theta_echo*beta_r,
    L_i=1+(lambda_echo/alpha0)*t_i,
    Z_i=(c_echo/alpha0)*t_i*Psi_r                    (19)

when i is either member of that pair. For middle and terminal queries
L_i=1,Z_i=0. Thus the full gradient Gram is

    <g_i,g_j>=sigma^2*(L_i L_j+<Z_i,Z_j>),           (20)

where every transverse product is(13) times its two signed factors.
Equivalently its coefficient on line L_a is
(c_echo/alpha0)*t_i*eta_a/R_U for a>=r, and zero otherwise.
No sign is lost by taking an expected covariance.

If X_echo=A_anchor e0-u0, with A_anchor=6 or7/2 according to(17), the exact initial
cross-products and norm are

    <X_echo,g_i>=sigma*(A_anchor L_i-<u0,Z_i>),
    ||X_echo||^2=A_anchor^2+1.                                 (21)

Every point-point and point-gradient Gram entry follows by substituting
x_i=X_echo-sigma D_(j(i)) and bilinearly expanding(20),(21). For example

    <x_i,g_j>=<X_echo,g_j>
                -sum_(t<j(i)) b_t <g_t,g_j>,
    <x_i,x_j>=||X_echo||^2-sum_(t<j(i))b_t<X_echo,g_t>
       -sum_(t<j(j))b_t<X_echo,g_t>
       +sum_(t<j(i),u<j(j))b_t b_u<g_t,g_u>.          (22)

In(22) a compressed g_t is intended inside the sums. Thus the ENTIRE
Gram of all original vectors is specified, with true initial correlation
and every positive/negative temporal coefficient. PSD is automatic from
the actual R^(N+1) vectors, not an optimizer-selected covariance.

### 4.2 One explicit common value array and ALL original directed rows

Define lower and upper adjacent prices, for t<k, by

    price_t^lo=b_t<v_(t+1)^echo,v_t^echo>+||v_(t+1)^echo-v_t^echo||^2/2,
    price_t^hi=b_t||v_t^echo||^2-||v_(t+1)^echo-v_t^echo||^2/2.     (23)

For A and B use rho_t=1/2 for every t. For C use rho_t=13/18 for
t<k-1 and rho_(k-1)=0. Put

    price_t=(1-rho_t)price_t^lo+rho_t price_t^hi,
    C_r^val=sum_(t=r)^(k-1)price_t, C_k^val=0,
    U_r^opt=sigma<v_r^echo,X_echo>
        -sigma^2*(C_r^val+<v_r^echo,D_r>+||v_r^echo||^2/2),
    L_r^opt=sigma^2*(||v_r^echo||^2/2-C_r^val),
    tau_val=min_(0<=r<=k) U_r^opt,
    fhat_r=tau_val+sigma^2 C_r^val,
    f_i=fhat_(j(i)).                                 (24)

This is a definite scalar rule on the specified vectors and physical
word, not arbitrary PSD covariance selection or a general PEP.
It specifies all values before the legality test. Neither convexity
of a price combination nor Gram positivity is asserted to prove legality.

Include x_star=g_star=f_star=0. The ORIGINAL residual orientation is

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.       (25)

For finite compressed i,j the exact ALL-row costs are

    Q_ij/sigma^2=C_i^val-C_j^val
                +<v_i^echo,D_i-D_j>+||v_i^echo-v_j^echo||^2/2.
    Q_(i,star)=tau_val-U_i^opt,
    Q_(star,i)=L_i^opt-tau_val.                       (26)

For original repeated queries use their j(i),j(j) indices. Their equal
jets make the corresponding residual0. Every remote, incoming-terminal,
outgoing-terminal and optimum row is retained in(26). Every directed
cycle is the SUM of these exact rows, with value terms telescoping.

The adjacent-cycle necessity is the exact identity

    Q_(t,t+1)+Q_(t+1,t)
      =sigma^2*[||v_(t+1)^echo-v_t^echo||^2
                     +b_t<v_(t+1)^echo-v_t^echo,v_t^echo>].         (27)

The complete optimum interval is
max_r L_r^opt<=tau_val<=min_r U_r^opt. The chosen tau_val is its
upper endpoint; legality still requires that the interval is nonempty
AND that every finite row in(26) is nonpositive.

The original optimum-to-terminal PATH is also retained. The row
Q_(0,star)<=0 and each Q_(t+1,t)<=0 give, for ANY legal common values,

    2f_n<=2sigma<v_0^echo,X_echo>-sigma^2 D_path,
    D_path=||v_0^echo||^2+2sum_(t<k)b_t<v_(t+1)^echo,v_t^echo>
                         +sum_(t<k)||v_(t+1)^echo-v_t^echo||^2. (27a)

This follows by adding those exact original rows; no quadrature or
positive-covariance shortcut replaces their ordered coefficients.
In terms of(23), D_path=||v_0^echo||^2+2sum_t price_t^lo. It is a necessary
path screen, not a sufficiency assertion. If D_path is nonpositive,
no completion-of-the-square upper bound is inferred from it.

For completeness the NEW first-cycle charge is explicit as well. For
k>=4 put H10=<Psi_1,u0>, H11=||Psi_1||^2, both given by(13). Its
longitudinal and transverse increments are

    dL=(lambda_echo/alpha0)*(alpha1-alpha0),
    dZ=(c_echo/alpha0)*(alpha1*Psi_1-alpha0*u0).

Then the first actual cycle is sigma^2 times

    dL^2+||dZ||^2+b0*((1+lambda_echo)*dL+c_echo<dZ,u0>),
    ||dZ||^2=(c_echo/alpha0)^2
                       *(alpha1^2 H11+alpha0^2-2alpha1 alpha0 H10),
    <dZ,u0>=(c_echo/alpha0)*(alpha1 H10-alpha0).       (27b)

Here alpha1 means alpha_1, not a fresh coefficient or a discarded
initial correlation. For k=3 the next query is the zero-memory middle,
and that cycle divided by sigma^2 is
lambda_echo^2+c_echo^2-b0*(lambda_echo*(1+lambda_echo)+c_echo^2).
For k=2 put d=1+theta_echo*b0/b1. The first charge is

    d*[d*(lambda_echo^2+c_echo^2)
                   -b0*(lambda_echo*(1+lambda_echo)+c_echo^2)]. (27c)

These formulas pay the first positive physical step even with leading
zeros. In the written b=(2,2) calibrations the first charges are0,-4,
-9/4 for A,B,C respectively. Thus the new failures are not inferred
by applying the old regular-simplex first-cycle formula.

### 4.3 Static realization and the true radius; no expected legality

ONLY IF every original row in(26) passes, the accepted construction
of fact1252b8823bb5a290 realizes these exact jets as ONE static function:

    K_h(v)=max{||v||^2/2,
       max_(0<=i<=n)[<x_i,v>-f_i+||v-g_i||^2/2]},
    F_h(x)=sup_v[<x,v>-K_h(v)].                       (28)

It is finite everywhere, convex differentiable, 1-smooth, nonnegative,
and attains minimum0 at0. The physical updates in(18) are its actual
GD trajectory because all queried gradients are interpolated. This
procedure never claims a static function for a failed law.

If additionally tau_val>max_r L_r^opt, then f_i>||g_i||^2/2 for
EVERY finite jet. At v=0 each nonzero-jet quadratic in K_h is strictly
negative while the zero-jet quadratic is0. Finitely many continuous
quadratics leave K_h(v)=||v||^2/2 in a neighborhood of0. Therefore
partial K_h(0)={0}. A minimizer x of F_h has gradient0, so x belongs
to partial K_h(0), and hence argmin F_h={0}. Its TRUE closest initial
distance is ||X_echo||, namely sqrt(37) for A/B and sqrt(53)/2 for C.
No unit initial radius is imposed after changing the geometry.

The same local identity shows F_h(x)=||x||^2/2 near0: for sufficiently
small x, v=x is the maximizing vector because x is the gradient of
K_h at v=x. Thus smoothness1 is sharp for the finite examples below.
If strictness fails, the existence of another closer minimum is not
ignored; the performance bound for B will use the ACTUAL closest
minimizer and does not need unique minimum.

For any advertised L>0 use L F_h with physical steps h_i/L. Points
are the same and normalized payoff is 2F_h(x_n)/dist(X_echo,argmin F_h)^2.
There is no randomization; every claimed realized member is admissible.

## 5. Law A: the remote cycle rejects nonzero transverse closed memory

For each pair, b_r alpha_r=b_(r') beta_r. Hence(16) gives the exact
full physical displacement identity

    sum_(r<k)b_r v_r^echo
      =S e0+(1-theta_echo)sum_(r<ell)b_r alpha_r Y_r. (29)

For theta_echo=1 the memory disappears from the TOTAL displacement.
In A, v_0^echo=e0+u0, v_k^echo=e0 and x_n-X_echo=-sigma S e0. The remote initial
and terminal pair then satisfies, by direct expansion of(25),

    Q_(0,n)+Q_(n,0)
      =||g_n-g_0||^2-<g_n-g_0,x_n-X_echo>
      =sigma^2||u0||^2=sigma^2>0.                   (30)

This holds for ALL common values, ALL anchors X_echo and ALL minima. It
also retains all earlier zero repetitions: original query0 has the
same initial gradient as compressed query0, even with leading zeros.
Changing the initial anchor or terminal values cannot pay this row.
Sigma=0 removes all nonzero memory and is outside the stated family;
no positive scale repairs (30).

### 5.1 Adjacent tests and optimum tests really can miss this defect

Use the same incidence memory on b=(2,2), for which Psi_0=u0 is unit.
In coordinates (e0,u0), the attempted gradients are

    v_0^echo=(1,1), v_1^echo=(1,-1), v_2^echo=(1,0).

At sigma=1 the first adjacent cycle(27) is4-4=0 and the second is
1-2=-1. Thus the first actual two-row cycle is PAID, unlike the
previous regular-simplex initial-offset failure.

To show that optimum rows do not fix the omission, translate the
diagnostic initial anchor to(10,0) and use values(f0,f1,f2)=(6,4,1).
The actual attempted points are(10,0),(8,-2),(6,0). In row/column
order(star,0,1,2), the COMPLETE original residual matrix is

    [ 0   -5    -3    -1/2
     -3    0     0     3/2
     -5    0     0    -1/2
     -9/2 -1/2  -1/2   0 ].                         (31)

For example Q02=6-1+(1,1).(-4,0)+1/2=3/2,
while Q20=1-6+(1,0).(4,0)+1/2=-1/2. All six optimum
directions and all four adjacent finite rows pass; the remote pair
has positive sum1. This is a counterexample to the SUFFICIENCY of
those truncated tests, not a claimed admissible function. The anchor
translation is only a diagnostic, not a fourth law or a radius grant.

## 6. Law B: longitudinal payment permits legality but exact closure caps it

B adds initial longitudinal surplus1, so v_0^echo=2e0+u0 rather than
e0+u0. It no longer has A's fixed positive remote-cycle obstruction.
Still theta_echo=1 gives from(29)

    sum_(t<n)h_t g_t=S*g_n,
    x_n=X_echo-S*g_n.                                    (32)

Consider ANY legal static realization of gradients with this identity,
allowing arbitrary values, anchors, dimensions and minimizers. Let
x_cl be a closest minimizer and R=||X_echo-x_cl||>0. Such a closest point
exists because the nonempty minimizer set of a finite continuous convex
function is closed, and a minimizing-distance sequence is bounded.
The original terminal-to-minimizer row gives

    2(f_n-f_star)
      <=2<g_n,x_n-x_cl>-||g_n||^2
      =2<g_n,X_echo-x_cl>-(1+2S)||g_n||^2
      <=R^2/(1+2S).                                 (33)

For the last inequality expand the nonnegative square
||X_echo-x_cl-(1+2S)g_n||^2. Dividing by R^2 yields

    TRUE normalized payoff <=1/(1+2S)=H_base(h).     (34)

This bound uses the true closest radius, not distance to a conveniently
selected origin. All longitudinal means and initial correlations in B
are paid by the ORIGINAL terminal row. No common-value choice, moving
minimum, metric amplitude or successful remote-cycle repair avoids it.
The conclusion is a consequence of EXACT(32), not a universal signed-
memory impossibility theorem. A law with nonzero terminal memory or
nonclosing weighted displacement is outside this scope.

### 6.1 A fully legal nonzero-memory finite calibration

For b=(2,2), sigma=1, X_echo=(6,-1) in(e0,u0) coordinates, B gives

    i    x_i          g_i       f_i
    0    (6,-1)       (2,1)     13/2
    1    (2,-3)       (0,-1)     5/2
    2    (2,-1)       (1,0)      3/2.                (35)

The actual updates subtract2g0 and2g1. The half-price convention in
(23),(24) gives price0=4, price1=1, C=(5,1,0). Its complete optimum
upper values are(7/2,3/2,3/2) and lower values(-5/2,-1/2,1/2),

Direct expansion of every original row, in order(star,0,1,2), gives

    [ 0  -4  -2  -1
     -2   0  -2  -2
      0  -2   0   0
      0   0   0   0 ].                               (36)

For instance Q01=4+(2,1).(-4,-2)+4=-2 and
Q02=5+(2,1).(-4,0)+1=-2. Every directed cycle is a sum of

The original path(27a) here has D_path=11 and <v_0^echo,X_echo>=11;
its necessary value inequality is also satisfied by these jets.

Consequently(28), using exactly these three jets plus the origin,
is ONE globally legal sharp1-smooth static function. Strict lower

In completely explicit form its fixed conjugate is

    K_B(v)=||v||^2/2+max{0,
                    4<v,e0>-2<v,u0>-4,
                    2<v,e0>-2<v,u0>-2,
                      <v,e0>-<v,u0>-1},
    F_B(x)=sup_v[<x,v>-K_B(v)].                       (36a)

Each affine intercept is -f_i+||g_i||^2/2 and each coefficient is
x_i-g_i from(35), so this is exactly(28), not a new data-dependent
function switch during the physical trajectory.
Its normalized payoff is3/37<1/9=H_base(2,2), as(34) requires.
The real field geometry here is still nonuniform by(10), even though
this smallest calibration uses one transverse mode.

### 6.2 Actual original benchmark at the unchanged hierarchy

For the assigned enclosing parameters define, without execution,

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta_word=1-1/(4m),
    c_word=lambda*(1+1/(4m)), a_word=beta_word/c_word,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_word*c_word^j,H_j),
    h=H_m, n_m=2^m-1, m>=1536.                       (37)

Every child uses the SAME enclosing-m parameters. The exact original
ALL-split benchmark is

    Omega(a,d)=(a+d+sqrt((a+d)^2+4ad))/2,
    G(a,d)=2a+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8ad)/2,
    C_0=B_0=1,
    C_n=max_(0<=j<n)Omega(C_j,C_(n-j-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=j<n)G(C_j,B_(n-j-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.  (38)

Accepted5fd396eba75651e7 Section10, equation(77), gives at THIS
actual full physical horizon

    B_(n_m)*H_base(H_m)<237/250.                      (39)

Therefore every legal B member has payoff/U_(n_m)^ZJ<237/250.
No new benchmark DP, substituted child-m parameter or truncated split
is used. This rejects the exact-balanced memory law as a universal

## 7. Law C: a legal over-retirement bridge and an unpaid terminal return

C changes theta_echo to2 and c_echo to1/2. Its total displacement
is, from the direct pair sum(29),

    sum_(t<n)h_t g_t
      =sigma*[S e0-sum_(r<ell)b_r alpha_r Y_r].       (40)

Thus (33) is NOT applied to C. Its incomplete cancellation can improve
the terminal value. The whole original terminal charge is retained by
(40), rather than asserted nonnegative or replaced by an expectation.
In fact, put M_ret=sum_(r<ell)b_r alpha_r/alpha0. Its terminal-optimum
row, at the EXACT anchor(17), is

    2f_n<=7sigma-sigma^2*(1+2S-2M_ret).              (40a)

The negative correction2M_ret is exactly the retained longitudinal
return in(40). Its sign does not license omitting a cycle or replacing
the true initial squared radius53/4 by1. No sign of the entire
quadratic coefficient in(40a) is assumed for an arbitrary word.

### 7.1 A complete static finite bridge with true radius

For b=(2,2), sigma=1, X_echo=(7/2,-1), C gives

    i    x_i           g_i         f_i
    0    (7/2,-1)      (2,1/2)     15/4
    1    (-1/2,-2)     (-1,-1)      3/2
    2    (3/2,0)       (1,0)        1.               (41)

All vectors are again written in orthonormal coordinates(e0,u0).
The first update subtracts(4,1), and the second subtracts(-2,-2),
so these are the actual physical points, not an independently selected
Gram. Its full weighted-incidence source is exactly(4)--(13).

The adjacent price bounds in(23) are

    price0^lo=5/8, price0^hi=23/8,
    price1^lo=1/2, price1^hi=3/2.

Thus rho0=13/18 and rho1=0 give price0=9/4, price1=1/2,
C=(11/4,1/2,0). The optimum upper values in(24) are(13/8,1,1),
and the lower values are(-5/8,1/2,1/2). Hence tau_val=1, exactly

In row/column order(star,0,1,2), ALL original residuals are

    [ 0      -13/8   -1/2   -1/2
     -5/8      0     -5/8   -1/8
      0      -13/8    0      -1
      0       -1/8    0       0 ].                   (42)

For example Q01=9/4+(2,1/2).(-4,-1)+45/8=-5/8,
and Q02=11/4+(2,1/2).(-2,1)+5/8=-1/8.
The remaining entries follow from the same stated formula(25);
no pair or optimum direction is omitted. Every directed cycle is a
sum of entries of(42) and is nonpositive.

The full original initial-optimum-to-terminal path has
D_path=13/2 and <v_0^echo,X_echo>=13/2. Its necessary inequality(27a) is
satisfied at sigma=1, and the exact terminal inequality(40a) is tight:
S=4,M_ret=2 give 2f_n<=7-5=2. Every payment uses the SAME data(41).

Consequently the concrete maximum of four quadratics in(28), with
the jets(41) and the zero optimum, yields one globally legal convex
sharp1-smooth static function. It has unique minimum0 by the strict
lower separation already checked. Its true squared radius is53/4.
Its fixed conjugate is

    K_C(v)=||v||^2/2+max{0,
                  (3/2)<v,e0>-(3/2)<v,u0>-13/8,
                  (1/2)<v,e0>-<v,u0>-1/2,
                  (1/2)<v,e0>-1/2},
    F_C(x)=sup_v[<x,v>-K_C(v)].                       (42a)

The three affine rows are precisely x_i-g_i and
-f_i+||g_i||^2/2 from(41), including the actual terminal jet.
The exact normalized objective is

    V_C(2,2)=8/53>1/9=H_base(2,2),                 (43)

since72>53. Initial gradient is unaligned with X_echo, the middle gradient
has a negative initial-radius component, and <g0,g1>=-5/2<0.
Specifically <g1,X_echo>=-5/2<0. Signed memory is an ACTUAL legal effect
in this finite example, not a negative formal covariance direction.

At actual n=2, the original recurrence(38) has the split j=0 giving
B1=G(1,1)=4 and B2>=G(1,B1)=G(1,4)=4+2sqrt(3)>7. Therefore

    V_C/U_2^ZJ=8B2/53>56/53>1.                     (44)

This finite bridge still does NOT improve the existing same-word
single-cut witness: acceptedb98bb807d514e80d, with first step2,
P=0,T=2, gives1/5, and8/53<1/5 since40<53.
No original all-word or asymptotic conclusion follows from(43),(44).

### 7.2 Last actual two-row cycle rejects the declared law on palindromes

For ANY positive palindrome, its first and last positive steps are
equal, say b>0. Thus alpha0=beta_0, Psi_0=u0 and (16) in C gives

    v_(k-1)^echo=e0-2*(e0+u0/2)=-e0-u0,
    v_k^echo=e0,
    v_k^echo-v_(k-1)^echo=2e0+u0.                              (45)

The actual last update is x_n-x_(last)=-b*sigma*v_(k-1)^echo.
By the exact ORIGINAL two-row cycle(27),

    Q_(last,n)+Q_(n,last)
      =sigma^2*[||2e0+u0||^2
                       +b<2e0+u0,-e0-u0>]
      =sigma^2*(5-3b).                               (46)

It is positive for EVERY sigma>0 when b<5/3. This is an exact
scale-family failure independent of initial anchors and ALL common
values. It remains true with original leading/trailing/interior zero
steps: choose the original last-positive query and original terminal;
the repeated zero queries neither change the point nor pay the cycle.

In the unchanged hierarchy(37), b=1+a_word and

    a_word=beta_word/c_word<1/lambda=sqrt(2)-1,
    b<sqrt(2)<5/3.                                  (47)

The second strict inequality follows by squaring the positive numbers,
2<25/9. Therefore every C scale member is UNREALIZABLE on H_m,
at every assigned m>=1536. This is not a small unrestricted W upper
bound on H_m. It is only the exact frozen terminal-ratio obstruction
for C. Changing these ratios or allowing terminal memory is outside
the conclusion, not already ruled out by(46).

## 8. What the changed geometry did, and what remains open

This construction leaves the old projective regular simplex by real
point weights with an exact full-field Gram and frame normalization.
It leaves the old parabolic and aligned-initial-gradient laws by signed
retired suffix memories and arbitrary initial transverse correlation.
B and C actually admit nonzero-memory finite static realizations;
there is no assertion that first-cycle payment always kills signed memory.

But a combinatorial change alone does not control the original directed
cycles or objective. A's early-to-late cancellation hides a positive
REMOTE cycle. B's added longitudinal payment fixes that local failure
in a finite example, but its exact overall displacement identity pays
all apparent objective gains at the terminal optimum row. C escapes
that identity and realizes a finite gain; it spends more than its last
small physical step can supply on the target diagnostic hierarchy.

These obstructions are invariant to the stated gradient scale but NOT
universal under a changed signed coefficient law. They exclude no
arbitrary nonuniform real point weights, arbitrary signed memory,
new initial anchors or terminal longitudinal reversals. In particular
the next interface must break EXACT(32), and a nonclosing return must
pay its actual last-step and remote cycles with newly derived coefficients.
Simply retaining expected sign or a terminal-row advantage is insufficient.

The original affirmative target still requires, for EVERY epsilon>0,
a threshold N(epsilon) independent of h such that for EVERY n>=N and
EVERY nonnegative physical word h there is one admissible static
function with attained minimum and TRUE closest-radius payoff at least
(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ. A finite bridge such as(41) is not this theorem.
A negative resolution still requires an all-function objective UPPER
bound with a fixed relative benchmark improvement on unbounded physical
horizons. No such upper bound is claimed here.

Any arbitrary-word decomposition, scalable signed return rule, and
uniform asymptotic original-B comparison for a law beyond these three
remain UNPAID THEOREM-LEVEL gaps. No target candidate exists to verify
in this allocated round. No finalization, revocation, publication,
shutdown, original-Goal completion or automatic renewal is authorized.

# Whole-field nonregularity for every positive nonuniform point metric

Status: JOINT UNVERIFIED appendix to max2 round61. No fact promotion,
formal gate or computation. This gives an all-weight argument in addition
to the explicit two-step metric demonstration in87225e1b68c94b70.
It is not used as a separately established premise.

Let p_wm be a prime, and put N=p_wm^2+p_wm+1. Points of the projective
plane over Z/p_wm Z are nonzero triples modulo nonzero scalars, and
lines are nonzero forms modulo scalars, with incidence by annihilation.
There are N points and N lines because each nonzero class has p_wm-1
representatives. A nonzero form has a two-dimensional kernel, giving
p_wm+1 points per line. Distinct line forms have a one-dimensional
common kernel, hence one intersection point. Dually each point is on
p_wm+1 lines and two distinct points share one line. All counts follow
by eliminating one or two coordinates with nonzero coefficients.

Let B be the real point-by-line incidence matrix. With I the identity,
J_ff the all-ones matrix and 1 the all-ones column, the point-side
counts give

    B B^T=p_wm I+J_ff, B1=(p_wm+1)1.                  (A1)

B is invertible since for nonzero z the quadratic form of B B^T is
p_wm||z||^2+(sum z)^2>0.

Give EVERY point P an arbitrary STRICTLY POSITIVE real weight omega_P.
Let W=diag(omega_P), Omega_w=sum_P omega_P, omega_max=max_P omega_P,
s_P=sqrt(omega_P), and P_w=I-s*s^T/Omega_w. Define every line column

    A_L=P_w W^(1/2)1_L/sqrt(p_wm*omega_max),
    A=(A_L)_L, G_wm=A^T A.                           (A2)

Thus the exact weighted Euclidean Gram has entries

    (G_wm)_LM=[sum_(P in L intersection M)omega_P
          -(sum_(P in L)omega_P)(sum_(P in M)omega_P)/Omega_w]
                                               /(p_wm*omega_max).

Using(A1), P_w*s=0 and W^(1/2)J_ff W^(1/2)=s*s^T gives the complete
field frame identity

    A A^T=P_w W P_w/omega_max.                       (A3)

Also A1=0. Conversely A c=0 implies Bc is constant, since
P_w W^(1/2)Bc=0 and the kernel of P_w is span(s). Invertibility of
B and B1=(p_wm+1)1 imply c is constant. Therefore

    ker A=span(1), range A=s perpendicular.           (A4)

Claim: the WHOLE-FIELD Gram G_wm is the Gram of a regular simplex
if and only if all omega_P are equal. Here regular simplex means

    G_wm=kappa_reg*(I-11^T/N) for some kappa_reg>0.   (A5)

This definition is equivalent to constant diagonal/off-diagonal entries
for the rank N-1 Gram with null column1: the row-sum condition forces
diagonal+(N-1)*off-diagonal=0, giving exactly(A5).

First suppose(A5). By A1=0,
A A^T A=A G_wm=kappa_reg A. By(A4), A A^T is multiplication by
kappa_reg on s perpendicular and zero on span(s); consequently
A A^T=kappa_reg P_w. Equation(A3) yields

    P_w(W-c_reg I)P_w=0, c_reg=kappa_reg*omega_max>0. (A6)

For any two distinct points i,j let
z_ij=e_i/s_i-e_j/s_j, where e_i,e_j are their coordinate unit vectors.
Then z_ij is perpendicular to s. Its quadratic form in(A6) is

    (omega_i-c_reg)/omega_i
                   +(omega_j-c_reg)/omega_j=0.      (A7)

Put zeta_i^wm=(omega_i-c_reg)/omega_i. Every distinct pair satisfies
zeta_i^wm+zeta_j^wm=0. Choose any three distinct indices, possible since N>=7.
The first two pair equalities give zeta_j^wm=zeta_k^wm=-zeta_i^wm; the third gives
-2zeta_i^wm=0. Thus zeta_i^wm=zeta_j^wm=zeta_k^wm=0. Pairing any other index with i gives
that index's zeta-value0 as well. Hence omega_i=c_reg for EVERY point.

Conversely if all omega_P equal c>0, then omega_max=c,
P_w=I-11^T/N and A=P_w B/sqrt(p_wm). The line-side counts give
B^T B=p_wm I+J_ff, and B^T1=(p_wm+1)1. Therefore

    G_wm=(1/p_wm)[B^T B-((p_wm+1)^2/N)11^T]
         =I-11^T/N,                                 (A8)

since N=p_wm^2+p_wm+1 makes 1-(p_wm+1)^2/N=-p_wm/N. This proves
the converse and identifies the normalization without a spectral
or finite-field-to-real shortcut.

Apply this argument to the round61 weights

    omega_P=1+1_(P=P0)
                +sum_(r<floor(k/2))b_r 1_(P in L_r)/(1+S),
    S=sum_(r<k)b_r, b_r>0, k>=2.

The sum over the first half is less than S, so every point other than
P0 has weight<2 whereas P0 has weight>=2. They are nonuniform.
Thus the complete real field Gram is NOT a regular simplex for EVERY
word to which the paired law applies, not only the written p_wm=2
calibration. This strengthens the geometry check, but changes none
of the three coefficient laws or their already joint-derived failures.
It gives no all-word lower witness, full-class upper bound or accepted
new fact. There is no extra mathematical program or formal submission.

Glossary: p_wm,N,P,L,B,I,J_ff,1 are the explicitly defined projective
prime, number of points/lines, points, lines, real incidence matrix,
identity, all-ones matrix and all-ones column. omega_P,W,Omega_w,
omega_max,s,P_w,A_L,A,G_wm are the real weighted construction(A2).
kappa_reg is the positive regular-simplex coefficient in(A5); c_reg
is kappa_reg*omega_max. e_i,z_ij,zeta_i^wm are the coordinate unit vectors,
test vectors and diagonal ratios in(A7). The final b_r,S,k,P0,L_r
are positive physical steps, their total, their count, a distinguished
point and the selected lines in the explicitly restated word weights.
